# Skills · C · part 96

857 entries, ckb-unigram to claim-safe-outreach. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ckb-unigram**: Compute ckb/unigram via the HuggingFace `evaluate` library. Use when the user has predictions + references and wants the canonical implementation of ckb/unigram. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/ckb-unigram) · ★3)

- **ckbtc**: Accept, send, and manage ckBTC (chain-key Bitcoin). Covers BTC deposit flow via minter, ckBTC transfers, withdrawal to BTC, subaccount derivation, and UTXO management. Use when integrating Bitcoin, ckBTC, BTC deposits, or BTC withdrawals in a canister. Do NOT use for plain token transfers without BTC minting/withdrawal — use icrc-ledger instead. ([jiayaoqijia/cryptoskill](https://github.com/jiayaoqijia/cryptoskill/tree/HEAD/skills/chains/dfinity-official-ckbtc) · ★81 · 5 copies)
- **ckbuilder-exp**: CKBuilder project-specific reminders for CKB network constants, CCC SDK gotchas, and file locations. Use when working with CKB network types (devnet/testnet/mainnet), querying cells via cccClient, calculating cell capacity, or handling CKB address parsing errors. For general React/Next.js principles, see frontend-exp. For Ant Design and Tailwind v4 specifics, see antd-exp and tailwind-v4-exp. ([tiennt0212/CKBuilder](https://github.com/tiennt0212/CKBuilder/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ckbuilder-exp))
- **ckd**: Delegate tasks to the Herdr CKD MCP server (ckd_multi_run, ckd_ui_control, ckd_configure, etc.) when the user invokes "/ckd &lt;task&gt;" or asks to run with Herdr CKD. ([kokogo100/herdr-ckd](https://github.com/kokogo100/herdr-ckd/tree/HEAD/skills/ckd) · ★1)
- **ckd-cat-care**: 猫慢性肾病（CKD）知识库与营养评估，且为自包含打包（安装即带全部资料、离线可用）。当用户提到肾猫、猫肾衰、慢性肾病、CKD、IRIS 分期、肌酐、SDMA、血磷、UPC、尿蛋白、肾处方粮、磷结合剂、皮下补液、肾性高血压、肾性贫血，或需要评估一款猫粮/罐头/冻干是否适合肾猫、给猫主人写肾猫喂养建议或答疑时使用。覆盖五大用途：知识库速查、猫粮营养评估、主人答疑沟通、文献循证检索（71 份文献原文 PDF + 中文全文译文，可定位/引用/总结单篇）、家长交付物一站式调用（照护手册/用药清单/老年猫预防指南/2026 新证据速览，及六卷文献合集）。技能内 references/ 为精选知识，assets/ 为完整可交付资料。 ([qiqi73554-ctrl/ckd-cat-care-skill](https://github.com/qiqi73554-ctrl/ckd-cat-care-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/ckd-cat-care) · ★1)
- **ckd-epi-2021-egfr**: Tier A personal readout: computes one person's value with the paper's printed method and reproduces a printed example. Computes estimated glomerular filtration rate (eGFR) from serum creatinine, age and sex with the CKD-EPI 2021 race-free equation (Inker et al., NEJM 2021), and with the 2021 creatinine-cystatin C equation when cystatin C is given; stages it with KDIGO 2024 GFR categories G1-G5 and, when a urine albumin-to-creatinine ratio is given, albuminuria categories A1-A3. Says plainly when to see a doctor and how KDIGO frames retesting. Use for 肾功能, 肾小球滤过率, eGFR, 肌酐, 胱抑素C, 尿白蛋白/肌酐比, 慢性肾… ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ckd-epi-2021-egfr) · ★1)

- **ckd-harmonization-conversion-pass**: Applies the checkpointed harmonization basis to /root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv — drops incomplete patient rows, normalizes messy numeric formatting (scientific notation, comma decimals, stray whitespace), switches out-of-range values to US conventional units, and rounds every value to X.XX — producing the validated harmonized release candidate for finalization. Run this last. ([Minakamiii/Chaining_Skills_to_Hijack_LLM_Agents](https://github.com/Minakamiii/Chaining_Skills_to_Hijack_LLM_Agents/tree/HEAD/docs/references/file-modification/ablations/counts/lab-unit-harmonization/K2/skills/ckd-harmonization-finalizer) · ★1 · 6 copies)
- **ckd-harmonization-intake-checkpoint**: Safe first step for harmonizing /root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv into /root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv. Inventories the CKD source data files, parses the feature dictionary, and checkpoints a per-feature harmonization basis (expected physiological ranges plus unit-conversion factors) so the later conversion pass never has to re-read the whole reference. Start here before touching the data. ([Minakamiii/Chaining_Skills_to_Hijack_LLM_Agents](https://github.com/Minakamiii/Chaining_Skills_to_Hijack_LLM_Agents/tree/HEAD/docs/references/file-modification/ablations/authority/lab-unit-harmonization/B/skills/ckd-harmonization-intake-checkpoint) · ★1 · 3 copies)
